- **tissue-enriched-ion-identification-wilcoxon**: Use when preprocessing a spatial metabolomics peak table matrix (X/Y pixel coordinates plus ion intensity columns) to separate tissue from background pixels, and you have lasso-defined tissue and background region annotations to compare per-ion intensity distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tissue-enriched-ion-identification-wilcoxon))
- **tissue-enriched-ion-proportion-analysis**: Use when you have a peak table matrix of pixels by ion intensities with spatial coordinates and need to quantify the proportion of ions with higher expression in a tissue region versus background, as in SMQVP's QVP3 module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tissue-enriched-ion-proportion-analysis))
- **tissue-expression-profile**: Retrieve measured human RNA expression across tissues and classify tissue specificity for a gene. Use when checking whether a drug target is expressed in the tissue of interest, whether off-target expression creates a safety signal, or when establishing tissue-level expression context. Do not use for predicted regulatory variant effects (use regulatory-variant-effect), protein structure confidence (use protein-structure-confidence), genetic constraint evidence (use target-genetic-evidence), or cell-type-resolved expression — this tool returns tissue-level averages only. ([GoogleCloudPlatform/LifeSciences](https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/LifeSciences/tree/HEAD/applications/open-agentic-science-engine/skills/tissue-expression-profile) · ★58)
- **tissue-mapping-rate-statistical-comparison**: Use when RNA-seq reads from multiple tissue groups have been aligned (e.g., STAR Log.final.out files) and you need to quantify whether mapping-quality metrics such as % uniquely mapped or % 'too short' reads differ systematically by tissue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-mapping-rate-statistical-comparison))
- **tissue-metadata-annotation**: Use when building or extending the tissueMASST reference index: public MS/MS files must be tagged with tissue-associated metadata (organism, tissue, disease status) before spectral search queries can be mapped to biological context. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tissue-metadata-annotation))
- **tissue-microarray-image-batch-processing**: Use when you need to execute the MIRIAM cell-segmentation pipeline over many TMA cores across two software implementations and quantify cross-implementation segmentation agreement per image. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tissue-microarray-image-batch-processing))
- **tissue-region-annotation-from-lipid-co-localisation**: Use when a processed MALDI/imzML spatial metabolomics dataset (e.g. murine urinary bladder) must be segmented into anatomical regions and each cluster annotated using known region-defining lipid m/z markers plus differentially expressed m/z peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tissue-region-annotation-from-lipid-co-localisation))
- **tissue-sample-cohort-selection**: Use when preparing multi-tissue RNA-seq genotype and transcript expression matrices (e.g. GTEx V7) for sQTL mapping and you must decide which tissues/samples pass a minimum sample-size threshold for powered within-tissue QTL testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-sample-cohort-selection))
- **tissue-separation-verification-in-ordination-space**: Use when a labeled metabolomics feature table (samples x features with tissue/group labels) needs a check that one group forms a distinct cluster in unsupervised PCA space, e.g. buccal gland vs. other tissues. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tissue-separation-verification-in-ordination-space))
- **tissue-sharing-stratification-of-causal-components**: Use when you have CAFEH z-mode results for genome-wide significant GWAS loci across multiple tissues (GTEx) and need to quantify whether tissue-specific vs tissue-shared eQTL components differ in colocalization with disease loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-sharing-stratification-of-causal-components))
- **tissue-spatial-analysis-pipeline-execution**: Use when you have raw IMC (protein imaging) and SIMS (metabolite imaging) data from tissue regions that require spatial co-registration, single-cell-level intensity quantification, and joint analysis of protein–metabolite relationships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tissue-spatial-analysis-pipeline-execution))
- **tissue-specific-chromatin-profile-aggregation**: Use when prioritizing non-coding GWAS variants in a tissue- or cell type-specific manner: you have per-variant SAD scores from DeepFun pre-trained models and need tissue-level aggregation plus a disease-by-tissue comparison such as a heatmap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-chromatin-profile-aggregation))
- **tissue-specific-clustering-visualization**: Use when you have per-sample tncRNA detection data (e.g. Supplementary sheets 5.1–5.4) across plant tissues and want to test whether tncRNA composition clusters by tissue via 2D non-linear embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-clustering-visualization))
- **tissue-specific-dataset-handling**: Use when benchmarking or deploying m6A site predictors on tissue-specific 41-nt RNA segment datasets from human, mouse, or rat, or when cross-tissue/cross-species comparisons require consistent dataset splits and encodings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-dataset-handling))
- **tissue-specific-driver-gene-prediction**: Use when somatic mutation data can be split by primary tissue of origin (tissues with &gt;1000 samples) and the goal is to predict cancer-type-specific driver genes rather than pan-cancer drivers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-driver-gene-prediction))
- **tissue-specific-feature-matrix-construction**: Use when COSMIC somatic coding mutation data can be split by primary tissue of origin and the goal is to identify tissue-specific TSGs/OGs by reusing a pan-cancer cTaG model on per-tissue feature matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-feature-matrix-construction))
- **tissue-specific-gene-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a differential alignment or ranking of genes across two tissue conditions needs biological validation against a curated set of known tissue-specific genes and GO terms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-gene-enrichment-analysis))
- **tissue-specific-gene-set-annotation**: Use when you have a table of recurrent chromatin state patterns from EpiAlign horizontal alignment and need to determine which patterns carry cell-type-specific characteristics via tissue-specific gene overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-gene-set-annotation))
- **tissue-specific-marker-database-matching**: Use when scRNA-seq data are already clustered and each cluster needs automatic, reproducible cell type annotation via a literature-backed tissue-specific marker database instead of manual marker inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-marker-database-matching))
- **tissue-specific-metabolite-enrichment-filtering**: Use when a negative-ion-mode LC-MS feature table spans multiple tissue groups and you need features with extreme, statistically controlled enrichment in one tissue versus every other group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tissue-specific-metabolite-enrichment-filtering))
- **tissue-specific-metabolite-filtering**: Use when a list of statistically significant up/down-regulated ion m/z values from an MSI region comparison needs putative metabolite IDs restricted to those plausible for the imaged tissue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tissue-specific-metabolite-filtering))
- **tissue-specific-metabolite-quantification**: Use when you have LC-IM-MS/MS raw data from multiple tissue samples and need to identify and quantify unsaturated sterol lipids at the isomer level (distinguishing double-bond position and stereochemistry). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-metabolite-quantification) · 2 copies)
- **tissue-specific-prior-integration**: Use when recovering missing proteins from a DDA-PASEF or similar proteomics expression matrix and the sample's tissue/cell line (e.g. lung/A549, HeLa) is known, so complex-based inference can be conditioned on tissue-specific complex confidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/tissue-specific-prior-integration))
- **tissue-specific-reference-matching**: Use when annotating scRNA-seq clusters by matching identified marker genes (e.g. from a paired one-against-all comparison) against a curated tissue-specific reference of literature-reported markers to filter pseudo markers and rank candidate cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-reference-matching))
- **tissue-specific-xgboost-model-training**: Use when you have a large cohort of paired bulk miRNA and mRNA matrices for one tissue (e.g., TCGA skin, breast, or kidney) and need a trained regression model to infer miRNA expression from mRNA-only profiles such as pooled scRNA-seq. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tissue-specific-xgboost-model-training))
